## R-AIRSPEED

**Airspeed sensor placement and accuracy** — The pitot sits outside the prop slipstream in cruise (>= 1 prop radius, 0.14 m, laterally clear of every disc edge, or ahead of the prop plane), and after ARSPD_AUTOCAL the airspeed reads within +/-1 m/s of GPS-derived true airspeed in a calm-air box pattern — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/tiebreak/DECISION.md#5.1


## R-ARM-KILL

**Arming and emergency stop** — Arming requires a hardware or RC arm switch and the full ARMING_CHECK set; an RC emergency-stop switch stops all four motors within 1 s from any mode; props are off for every bench test of the electrics — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md#D8


## R-AVIONICS-POWER

**Avionics power** — The UBEC/BEC continuous rating >= 2 x the summed avionics current plus both servos at stall; the flight-controller rail stays >= 4.8 V with both servos stalled on the bench; the current and voltage sensor reads within +/-5 % of a meter at 24 A — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P18


## R-BATT-HANDLING

**Battery handling** — Packs are stored and carried at storage charge (3.80-3.85 V/cell) in a LiPo bag; charged attended on a non-flammable surface with sand or an extinguisher at hand, never in a car; inspected for puffing before every charge; bought from US stock and shipped by ground — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md#D12

## R-CONFIG

**Configuration K09-L** — Quad tailsitter: rectangular flying wing, reflexed section, two fins at +/-0.32 m each carrying a vertical CF tube with a motor at each end (4 motors in X), stands on the fin tips, two elevons, airspeed sensor; no pusher, no tilt, no structural field joint — **CHECKED**  
Defined in: redesign/CONFIGURATION.md

- claim **CL-A1-WING-AREA** Wing reference area = **0.345 m2** — **CHECKED**
  - how to verify: Measure the hot-wired core: span x chord.
  - calc `CALC-A1-WING` `redesign/team/A1/frame_claims.py::wing_geometry()`: `S = b c ; AR = b^2 / S` — **CHECKED**
    - `b` ← param `P:wing.span_m` = 1.15 m — **CHECKED**
      - source `SRC-K09-CARD` K09 concept card and sizing script, redesign/config-study/cards/K09/{CARD.md, sizing.py, sizing.json, cost.csv}; geometry constants in sizing.py ASSUMPTIONS block, checked 2026-11-09 — **CHECKED**
    - `c` ← param `P:wing.chord_m` = 0.3 m — **CHECKED**
      - `SRC-K09-CARD` (see above)

## R-CRASH-REPAIR

**Crash repair** — A crashed wing core is re-cut and skinned in <= 1 day from stock on hand (>= 1 spare XPS sheet, hot-wire templates and printed-part STLs kept in the repo); the BOM holds spares: 1 motor, 4 ESCs, 8 props, 1 CF tube, 4 feet — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P12


## R-CRUISE-AUTHORITY

**Elevon authority in cruise** — At 1.3 Vs with the CG at its aft limit, the elevons trim level flight using <= 50 % of the +/-25 deg travel, and the remaining travel gives a steady roll rate >= 90 deg/s (AVL with roll damping); pitch recovers a 10 deg upset without stalling — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/tiebreak/DECISION.md#5.1


## R-DIRECTIONAL

**Directional stability in cruise** — The plank wing with its two fins is directionally stable in cruise: Cn_beta >= +0.03 /rad at 1.3 Vs with elevons neutral and the CG at its aft limit (AVL, fins modelled as vertical surfaces at +/-0.32 m); the Dutch-roll mode is damped (no growing yaw oscillation in a hands-off FBWA run) — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/team/J1/CHANGE-REQUESTS.md#CR-J1-09

## R-FAILSAFES

**Failsafes and geofence** — RC loss -> QLAND or QRTL; GCS loss (if telemetry is used) -> as configured; battery low -> return, critical -> land; Q_TRANS_FAIL -> QRTL; geofence = the RFS sub-field polygon plus the 400 ft ceiling with a fence action; each action is demonstrated in SITL and on the bench (props off, transmitter switched off, voltage simulated) before the first flight — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md#D10

## R-FC-STABLE

**Stable autopilot build** — Flight controller runs a stable (not beta) ArduPilot Plane build with tailsitter support, logging to SD or flash — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P15


## R-FIELD-SETUP

**Field set-up time** — From the closed box to the arm check in <= 5 min by one person with no tools: fit four props, seat and strap the pack, close the pack plug, arm check; the sequence is the checklist in the box — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P9


## R-FIN-TUBE-JOINT

**Fin-tube-saddle joint** — The fin, motor tube and saddle transfer the motor-station loads (1.58 kgf per motor at full thrust, plus the landing case) across the 0.18 m tube-to-spar offset with margin >= 0 at ultimate, using printed clips and epoxy on a real 22 mm tube; a printed clip is fit-checked on the tube before the December order — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P25


## R-FSW-PARAMS

**Tailsitter parameter file** — The parameter file in subsystems/flight-software/ sets Q_ENABLE 1, Q_TAILSIT_ENABLE 1, Q_TAILSIT_MOTMX for all four motors, Q_FRAME_CLASS 1, Q_FRAME_TYPE 1, Q_OPTIONS bit 18 (arm only in VTOL); the output map matches stations UR/UL/LR/LL and their rotation directions; every parameter name is checked against the installed firmware version named in the file header — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/params/params.json#avionics.firmware


## R-GUST-LEAN

**8 m/s gust survive-and-land** — The hover attitude needed to hold position in an 8 m/s gust (flat-plate method, wing broadside) is <= Q_A_ANGLE_MAX minus 5 deg (F4 sets Q_A_ANGLE_MAX accordingly), and the thrust needed at that lean (W / cos lean) leaves a thrust headroom >= 1.3 at the gate mass with voltage sag — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P8


## R-HARNESS-INSTALL

**Wiring is an installation** — Every wire in the wire list has a route, supports <= 0.15 m apart, chafe protection at every edge, strain relief at every connector and a service loop across each elevon hinge; the ESC-to-motor leads run along the fin; no connector carries structural load; the pack plug is the only field disconnect — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/inputs/ADVISOR-XV24-LESSONS.md#3


## R-HOVER-AUTHORITY

**Hover control authority** — At the gate mass in hover, motor-differential control gives an angular acceleration on each body axis >= 2 rad/s2 and >= 1.5 x the acceleration the 5 m/s wind moment demands, using <= 30 % of hover thrust per motor as differential; yaw (about the hover vertical, from prop reaction torque) is the axis to show — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P8

## R-JOINT-STACK

**Joint stacks** — Every fastened or bonded joint in the README joint table has a joint-stack entry: fastener size x length and grade or adhesive, hole or insert, thread engagement >= 1.0 x diameter in a heat-set insert (or >= 2.0 x in bare plastic), torque or 'snug + threadlocker', tool, access direction, install and removal order — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/inputs/ADVISOR-XV24-LESSONS.md#2


## R-LANDING-IMPACT

**Fin-tip landing** — A vertical landing at 1.5 m/s sink (3 x the 0.5 m/s Q_LAND_SPEED default) with up to 10 deg attitude error is absorbed with no damage beyond the four replaceable feet; a foot is replaced in <= 10 min in the field with a knife and CA — **UNVERIFIED** **FAILS**

## R-LEGAL

**FAA and university compliance** — Take-off mass < 25 kg (covered by R-MASS-GATE); FAA registration done and the number displayed on the airframe; each pilot holds a TRUST certificate and AMA membership; aircraft and pilots registered with the UC Drone Program under a PI sponsor; the FAA UAS facility map for RFS checked once and the airspace class recorded — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md#D1


## R-LOADS

**Load cases defined** — C1 publishes numbered load cases for every structure before sizing: flight limit load factor +3.0 / -1.5 at the gate mass with ultimate = 1.5 x limit; hover at maximum thrust (4 x 1.58 kgf) at the motor stations; transition (max thrust plus 1.3 Vs aero load); fin-tip landing at 1.5 m/s sink with 10 deg attitude error on one foot pair; tip-over onto a wingtip; transport (0.3 m box drop); handling (one-hand carry at the pod) — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/TEAM.md#C1

**Every part connected** — Every part in every interfaces.json has at least one structural interface and, where it carries current or heat, an electrical or thermal one; interfaces/merge_check.py and fusion/check.py pass with zero floating parts and zero unacknowledged edges — **UNVERIFIED**

## R-SCHEDULE

**Milestones** — Twin flown before the order; electronics and CF tubes ordered by about 2026-11-20, everything else by 2026-12-15; first XPS hover by build week 4; first transition by week 7; complete by 2027-04-30 with one crash-repair loop (>= 1.5 weeks calendar) and the 7-business-day RFS request lead inside the plan — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P11


## R-SERVO-TORQUE

**Elevon servo torque** — Each elevon servo's rated stall torque >= 1.5 x the summed hinge moment of its two panels at 25 deg deflection at the dive speed 1.3 x cruise (19.5 m/s), hinge moment from XFOIL (B2); the linkage holds that load without the horn or clevis slipping — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P16


## R-SITE

**Site rules and flight card** — Flights only at RFS yellow sub-fields 1-4, within 06:00-sunset (flight card 07:00-10:00), <= 400 ft AGL, VLOS <= 500 m from the pilot, with a spotter, a request >= 7 business days ahead and a post-flight report within 3 business days; no Remote ID module while every flight is inside the FRIA (a conditional line stays in the BOM) — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/requirements/REQUIREMENTS.md#D5

## R-STRUCT-MARGIN

**Structural margins** — Every primary load path (spar, spar-slot bond, motor tube, tube-to-spar saddle, fin-tube joint, fin root bond, motor mount, pod-to-wing bond, pack retention, feet) has a margin of safety >= 0 at ultimate load (1.5 x limit) against stated material allowables, and the spar tip deflection at limit load <= 5 % of the semi-span — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/README.md#joints-both-sides-of-every-interface


## R-TEST-ORDER

**Test gates in order** — Gates passed and logged in order, none skipped: (1) twin (R-TWIN); (2) thrust stand, one station (R-HOVER-TW); (3) XPS aircraft weigh-in (R-MASS-GATE) and tethered hover with current within +/-15 % of 24 A; (4) untethered QHOVER/QLOITER; (5) fixed-wing tune as a plain flying wing; (6) first transition at >= 50 m AGL with QSTABILIZE recovery rehearsed; (7) back-transition at altitude; (8) landing practice in calm air; (9) full mission (R-MISSION). H3 gives go/no-go on the 5 m/s flight card — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/tiebreak/DECISION.md#5.4


## R-THERMAL

**Thermal limits** — Motors, ESCs, pack and avionics stay within rating for the full mission; the field-ambient ceiling is reported — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/REQUIREMENTS-PROPOSED.md#P19

## R-TIPOVER

**Stance tip-over** — Static tip-over angle about the narrow axis of the four-foot stance >= 30 deg (PROPOSED: exceeds the 16 deg hover lean at 5 m/s plus landing attitude error; C5 re-derives) — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/params/params.json#requirements.tipover_min_deg

- claim **CL-A1-TIPOVER** Tip-over angle, narrow axis = **37.23 deg** (limit >= 30 deg, margin +24%) — **CHECKED**
  - how to verify: Stand the aircraft on a tilting board (C5 test): record the angle at which it tips about the fin axis.
  - calc `CALC-A1-TIPOVER` `redesign/team/A1/frame_claims.py::tipover()`: `h = c - x_cg + foot ; narrow = atan(z_fin / h) ; wide = atan(y_fin / h)` — **CHECKED**
    - `c` ← param `P:wing.chord_m` = 0.3 m — **CHECKED**
      - source `SRC-K09-CARD` K09 concept card and sizing script, redesign/config-study/cards/K09/{CARD.md, sizing.py, sizing.json, cost.csv}; geometry constants in sizing.py ASSUMPTIONS block, checked 2026-11-09 — **CHECKED**
    - `x_cg` ← param `P:cg.x_target_m` = 0.06 m (A1 choice) — **PROPOSED**
    - `foot` ← param `P:cg.foot_height_m` = 0.01 m (A1 choice) — **PROPOSED**
    - `z_fin` ← param `P:fins.half_height_m` = 0.19 m — **CHECKED**
      - `SRC-K09-CARD` (see above)
    - `y_fin` ← param `P:fins.y_m` = 0.32 m — **CHECKED**
      - `SRC-K09-CARD` (see above)
  - param `P:requirements.tipover_min_deg` = 30 deg (A1 choice) — **PROPOSED**
- claim **CL-B1-GROUND-TIP-WIND** Wind that tips the parked aircraft = **9.581 m/s** (limit >= 8 m/s, margin +20%) — **UNVERIFIED**
  - ⚠ uses CL-B1-HOVER-TW-SAG, which is UNVERIFIED
  - how to verify: Tilt-board tip angle (C5) and a fan test at the lightest flying mass; never leave it standing disarmed in gusts.
  - calc `CALC-B1-WIND` `redesign/team/B1/performance.py::wind()`: `lean = atan(0.5 rho V^2 S CD90 / W) ; lean_allowed = tipover - err ; V_hover,max: lean = lean_allowed ; T/W at 8 m/s = 1/cos(lean) ; V_ground,tip = sqrt(2 m_min g z_fin / (h_cp rho S CD90)), h_cp = c/2 + foot` — **CHECKED**

Low-AR wings with end plates keep more post-stall lift, so this under-predicts lift in transition (conservative)) — **PROPOSED**

## R-TWIN

**Foam-board twin gate** — The foam-board twin on the real motors, FC and pack holds position within 1 m in calm air and in a 5 m/s fan or breeze with logged lean <= 20 deg; flies QLOITER at >= 30 m; completes a transition at >= 15 m/s, two laps and a back-transition; hover current within +/-15 % of 24 A and cruise near 13 A; the elevon size that gives clean FBWA authority is recorded. A wind fail triggers the K11 planform test, then the K01 fallback (CONFIGURATION.md) — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/config-study/tiebreak/DECISION.md#8


## R-VIBRATION

**Motor-tube vibration** — The first bending and torsion natural frequencies of each motor tube plus fin assembly with motors fitted lie outside +/-20 % of the 1P frequency at hover rpm and of 2P (blade pass), or a tap test shows the mode damped; the flight-controller vibration (ArduPilot VIBE) stays < 30 m/s2 in hover with Clip = 0 — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/TEAM.md#C4


## R-WIND

**Wind** — Hover and fin-tip landing controllable in 5 m/s steady wind; 8 m/s gust is survive-and-land — **UNVERIFIED** **FAILS**

## R-WIND-MEASURE

**RFS wind measured** — RFS morning wind is measured with a handheld anemometer on >= 3 mornings at 2 m height against the RCMC1 real-time reading; the ratio RFS/RCMC1 is < 1.4 (else the K01 reversal in CONFIGURATION.md applies) and the flight-card limit is rescaled to the measured ratio — **UNVERIFIED**  
Defined in: redesign/CONFIGURATION.md#reverse-to-k01

- claim **CL-J1-FIN-YAW-ARM** Fin centroid aft of CG = **0.04 m** — **CHECKED**
  - how to verify: B3: AVL Cn_beta with fins, tubes and motors; must be positive with margin. Flight: FBWA yaw doublet on the twin.
  - source `SRC-J1-HEPPERLE-PLANK` [M. Hepperle: Basic design of flying wing models (plank)](https://www.mh-aerotools.de/airfoils/flywing1.htm), unswept wing: NP at c/4; stability coefficient typically 0.02-0.05; trim needs Cm = CL x sigma, checked 2026-11-09 — **CHECKED**
  - source `SRC-J1-BOXKITE-LEARN` [dpapworth: BoxKite VTOL learning (4 student aircraft, logs)](https://discuss.ardupilot.org/t/boxkite-vtol-learning/139972), failed first transition; later all four yawed uncommanded in FBWA: Plane does not stabilise yaw in FW by default, checked 2026-11-09 — **CHECKED**
  - calc `CALC-J1-FIN-YAW-ARM` `redesign/team/J1/research_claims.py::fin_yaw_arm()`: `arm = (x_le + x_te)/2 - x_cg ; ahead = (x_cg - x_le)/(x_te - x_le) ; arm_c = arm / c` — **CHECKED**

    - `x_te` ← `P:fins.te_x_m` (see above)
    - `x_cg` ← `P:cg.x_target_m` (see above)
    - `c` ← `P:wing.chord_m` (see above)
    - `SRC-J1-HEPPERLE-PLANK` (see above)

  - how to verify: B3: include propeller normal force (Ribner) in the AVL/hand pitch and yaw stability sums; 0.7 chord ahead of the CG is destabilising in both axes.

  - calc `CALC-J1-PROP-ARM` `redesign/team/J1/research_claims.py::prop_arm()`: `arm = x_cg - x_prop ; arm_c = arm / c` — **CHECKED**
